# Jordyn's first car
_Safety first, then cost to own · 130 mi/week · $15k target, searched to $22k_

**171 listings** · updated 2026-09-04

> This is a plaintext export of a private family car roster, generated
> nightly from live dealer inventory. Prices and availability change daily;
> always confirm against the listing URL before acting on anything here.

Every car we can find that could work as Jordyn's first car — 4728 listings searched across a 250-mile radius, no model list, nothing ruled out by make. The “What the data says” tab has the answers: which models hold up, whether electric really beats petrol once you compare like with like, and whether spending past the $15k target buys anything. The shortlist below is a handful of cars per question — cheapest to own, best electric, best under budget, strongest safety — rather than a wall of near-identical listings. Browse everything if you want the long tail. Cars are ranked on total cost to own, not sticker price: fuel or charging, teen-driver insurance, maintenance, Washington's EV fee, depreciation, and a major-repair reserve built the same way for every powertrain. Tap 👍/👎 and add notes, then hit "Send my picks."

## How to read this

- Powertrain is resolved from the **VIN** via the NHTSA vPIC database, never
  from the model name. Model names lie: a "Hyundai Ioniq" may be a hybrid, a
  plug-in hybrid, or a battery EV, and only the VIN distinguishes them.
- Any car whose powertrain could not be VIN-verified is marked ⚠️. Its range
  and fuel costs are assumptions, not facts.
- Reliability is model-year level, triangulated from free public NHTSA data.
  It is NOT a purchased J.D. Power or Consumer Reports score, and it says
  nothing about the condition of the specific car — get a pre-purchase inspection.

## Cost assumptions

- Driving: 130 mi/week (6,760 mi/year)
- Horizon: 2 years for Jordyn, 6 years if Emma inherits it
- Gasoline: $6.00/gal (Bellevue, WA), premium $6.75/gal where the engine requires it
- Electricity: $0.239/kWh over 2 yr, $0.263/kWh over 6 yr
  (PSE Schedule 7 **Tier-2 marginal** rate — the cost of the next kWh, excluding the fixed $7.49/mo basic charge, which is paid whether or not you own an EV. Rising from $0.2356/kWh in 2027.)
- WA EV surcharge: $150/yr for plug-ins with 30+ mi of electric range (shorter-range plug-ins owe nothing)
- WA tabs: 1.1% Sound Transit RTA excise on 85% of ORIGINAL MSRP (not the price paid), depreciated by the 1990 statutory schedule, plus base/weight fees
- Sales tax: 10.8%
- Teen insurance: $—/yr + NaN% of vehicle value
- Maintenance per mile: BEV $0.04 · PHEV $0.06 · hybrid $0.06 · gas $0.09

> Insurance is the largest single line and is an estimate, not a quote.
> It has not been checked against a real teen-driver policy.
